Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the devices structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 946. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 432 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the devices space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 946 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 432. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the devices space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 83 and the convergence rate is O(841/n^4).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 83 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the devices structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 841. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
